How to Use This Document
This document serves as a reference for the Preference Ranking annotation project. 
This document is interactive. You can read this from start to finish or jump to different 
topics using the Navigation Bar on the left.

Introduction Overview
Users reach out to digital assistants for various reasons: to ask for specific information, to give instruction (e.g., create a passage, write a 
code), or simply to chat. Because of that, the majority of user requests are conversational and might be filled with colloquialisms, idioms, or 
unfinished phrases. Just like in human-to-human interaction, a user might comment on the digital assistant’s response or ask a follow-up 
question. While a digital assistant is very capable of generating human-like conversations, the limitations are still present. For example, it is 
challenging for the assistant to judge how accurate or safe (not harmful) the response is. This is where your role as an analyst comes into 
play. The purpose of this project is to evaluate digital assistant responses to ensure they are relevant, accurate, concise, and safe.

Introduction Overview
Ta s k Sub-T ask Description
User Request Analysis a. Interpret User request Analyze the user request, ensure understanding (using context if present)
b. Assess your ability to rate the 
response
The user request and response might need advanced specialized skills to 
provide reliable ratings. The presentation of the user request/response 
might have visual issues preventing the rating of the task.
Response Evaluation a. Provide ratings for each response You will be shown at least 2 responses. At this stage you will provide 
ratings for each response (you might be shown a subset of rating 
questions).
b. Compare responses You will then go through a series (if more than 2 responses are shown) of 
comparisons, provide your rating for which side you prefer.
Comments N/A Leave a detailed comment why one side was preferred to another OR if 
they are same.

1 User Request In the tool, the first section you will find is the User Request.
2 Predicted CategoryThe predicted category just below the user request shows the task's domain. This information will assist you in determining 
whether to skip the task due to an "expertise mismatch."
3 Report a Problem The Report a Problem icon is used to skip the task if the skipping requirements are met. Ensure that this feature is used 
responsibly and only when necessary!

2.Click the Report T ask button                                                                3. Select the reason for reporting a task and submit it:

To o l  U I UI Description
4 Responses A computer-generated response that needs to be evaluated.
5 Response EvaluationA set of response-related questions to be answered following the guidelines, before submission.  
A summary table of your responses by dimension will also populate as you make your selection. This will help assess the 
Satisfaction rating for the response.
6 Preference Ranking Assess, compare, and rate two responses to determine which one is better. 
A summary table of your dimension selection for each response will populate here. This will help you assess which response is 
better, based upon those selections.
7 Comments Explain your ranking in the comments. Be specific on why one response is better than the other.
8 Submit Button Use this button to submit the current task and move on to the next task.
9 Project Guidelines Project guidelines are linked on the top right side of the tool.

RESOURCES 
The purposes of this step are: 
•T o understand user intent and the underlying purpose of the request by: 
-Identifying the type of the user request and the context around it. 
•T o model the desired response - the ideal response that a user might expect. 
•T o identify whether the request is safe and doesn’t introduce potential harm.

1.1 Skipping Request
First, read the request and decide whether you can move forward with the evaluation. In rare cases, you might face the issues listed in the 
table below., which prevent you from evaluating the prompt or response.  
⚠ NOTE: These exceptional reasons for skipping the task apply to both, request and response. Hence, you may need to skip the task 
either in the User Request Evaluation step or later in the Response Evaluation step.
Step 1. User Request Evaluation

Skipping Reason When to use Examples
T echnical Issues A technical issue prevents you from 
correctly submitting the task.
Skip if: 
• Problem with the UI prevents you from making the correct grading selections. 
• Data did not load properly. The user request, predicted category, or a model’s 
response are missing. 
Do NOT skip if: 
• Sequense of letters is incorrect. For example, you may only see responses A, C, 
D, F listed. It's expected that sometimes letters will be missing, so do not skip.

Skipping Reason When to use Examples
Other Other reasons for 
reporting/skipping the 
task include: 
▪ Gibberish: The user 
prompt is gibberish / 
has no coherent 
meaning. 
Note: Vague or 
ambiguous requests 
should NOT be 
reported. 
▪ Language: The 
prompt or request is 
in the language in 
which you don't 
have expertise.  
Note: Do not report/skip 
the task if the Prompt 
is in English.
Skip if: 
• User request treats made up words / sounds as real words: "And hooptiously drangle me with crinkly 
bindlewurdles".
Do NOT skip if: 
• User request is vague: “where is the best place". 
• User request acknowledges a word is made up: "Write me a version of 'We wish you a merry 
christmas' except it's about a fictional 'Hempmas' which is a hemp and cannabis themed holiday" 
• User request is incomplete: "summarize this article for me". 
◦In this case the user would be expecting the assistant to follow up on which article to be 
summarized. 
Skip if: 
• The request is in language other than your locale: 
◦ "lister les 10 meilleurs outils de gestion du cycle de vie des contrats, applications saas sur le 
marché et donner un résumé pour l'étude de marché" (fr_FR locale). 
◦ ඇ८ճάϥϑͷྫͰ͢ ɻ(DAG) ex1 &lt;- dagitty("dag { X [exposure] Y [outcome] U 
[unobserved] Z -&gt; X -&gt; Y X &lt;- U -&gt; Y }") 気温ͱ⾼度ͷ変化ͷ DAG ੒͠·͢ ɻ
(ja_JP locale) 
Do NOT skip: 
◦ The user request is asking to translate a foreign phrase to your locale. For example, for en_US 
grading: "please translate 'Cómo está usted' to English".

STEP 3. COMMENTS

Skipping Reason When to use Examples
Expertise Mismatch You don’t have the expertise to 
assess the user request and 
responses, even with thorough 
online research.
Skip if: 
• You have limited knowledge in a certain field (e.g., mathematics, legal, etc.) and do 
not feel confident to rate the quality of the response. 
◦User request: "Prove that if the inequality is true for 𝑛 numbers, it is true for 2𝑛 
numbers". 
◦User request: "In the United States, do individuals have a legal right to not have 
their lives written about in a book that is available to the public?” 
⚠ NOTE: This doesn't mean any request related to mathematics, legal, etc. can be 
skipped. Do your best to proceed with evaluation. 
Research Time: 
Generally we should not spend more than 5 minutes per response on research. For example, if there are 3 responses for a specific user request, the 
total time for research collectively should not exceed more than 15 minutes in general.  
For more difficult or longer tasks, you may spend more time (~10 minutes). For extremely difficult tasks where you don’t think you can gain 
reasonable familiarity even with 10 minutes research, you may skip them by choose “Expertise Mismatch” . 
Important: Instances where the nature of the request requires you to spend more time researching to verify the given information, please proceed 
with evaluation and DO NOT SKIP such requests. For example, a user request asking you to verify 15 websites and provide a summary of each with a 
list of the best selling products.

Types of User Requests 
A user’s request can be of various types. The following table has some examples. The column “User Request Type” is not exhaustive and a user 
request can belong to several different user request types.
1.2 Request Type and Context
Examples of User Requests and Responses 
User Request Type Example User Request Notes
Questions & Answers
What year did World War II end? Q&A using digital assistant’s knowledge
Which player has the most home runs in the World Series in 
MLB history?
What is the Empty Day about and who were the cast 
members? Use only the provided text to answer 
"The Empty Day" is the 22nd television play episode of the first 
season of the Australian anthology tsome craf….."
Q&A about some text given to the digital assistant.
Math & Reasoning
Alice is three times as old as Bob, and Bob is 10 years older 
than Cindy. The sum of their ages is 80. How old is each 
person? Please provide a step-by-step explanation and show 
your work.
Usually solving math or logic/reasoning puzzles
What is the total of every number from 1 to 100 added 
together? Show me how you came up with that answer. Come 
up with a trick to make it easier to figure out.
Math problem with two instructions: reasoning 
behind the answer and the request for a ‘simple 
trick’

STEP 3. COMMENTS

Context 
As mentioned, understanding user requests is one of the key parts of the Response Evaluation step. In some tasks, you will see a piece of user-
assistant conversation (when a user is asking follow-up questions). The response you’ll be rating is supposed to fully address the last request from the 
user, taking all previous interactions into account. The last request may or may not be related to the previous conversation. In other words, to evaluate 
the response, you need to understand the request. Some requests can be easily understood while others require additional context. Consider the 
examples below. 
#Use-Assistant Interaction Does it require context and is 
the context present?
Comment Types of User Follow-Up 
Requests
1User: What is the capital of T exas 
Assistant: The capital of T exas is Austin 
User: How about Washington state
Context needed It is difficult to understand user intent 
without the context. However, the context 
implies that the last user request is referring 
to something previously mentioned and 
requesting the capital of Washington state.
Follow-up request based 
on previous requests
2User: "How important is consistency and 
dedication in this fitness goal" 
Assistant: "Consistency and dedication 
are very important for achieving a fitness 
goal [the response truncated]" 
User: "How do I reduce body fat and 
increase muscle mass?"
Context not needed The purpose of the user request is clear 
without the previous context. Although the 
follow-up question is related to the same 
topic, it makes sense as an independent 
request.

provide me with the text you would like summarized." 
User: "Offside is one of the laws in association football, codified in Law 11 of the Laws of the 
Game. The law states that a player is in an offside position if any of their body parts, except 
the hands and arms, are in the opponents' half of the pitch, and closer to the opponents’ 
goal line than both the ball and the second-last opponent (the last opponent is usually, but 
not necessarily, the goalkeeper). Being in an offside position is not an offence itself, but a 
player so positioned when the ball is played by a teammate can be judged guilty of an 
offside offense if they receive the ball or will otherwise become “involved in active play", will 
“interfere with an opponent", or will 
"gain an advantage" by being in that position. Offside is often considered one of the most 
difficult to understand aspects of the sport."
Context needed The last user request cannot be 
understood without the previous 
interaction between user and 
assistant.
Follow-up 
request to 
provide 
additional 
information, 
refinement, or 
extra 
requirements

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Attention to details 
The final step of the User Request Evaluation is to model the expected (ideal) response to this request. It is a best practice to put yourself 
in a user's shoes and try to imagine, what response you would expect from the assistant if you were to issue the specific request. 
T o model the expected response, read the request carefully and look for the specific instructions that should be reflected in the response. 
Ask yourself: 
1.Does the user indicate the format of the response (e.g., “provide me a bullet list of…” , etc.)? 
2.Does the user specify the length of the request (e.g., “give me 2 reasons why…” , “in 2 or 3 sentences summarize…” , etc.)? 
3.Is there any indication of a desired style (e.g., “write a small poem in a Shakespearian style” , “create a funny story about…” , etc.)? 
4.What specific action does the user expect (e.g., “summarize” , “rephrase” , “adjust the tone” , “write a bubble sort code in Python 
language, do not use comments” , etc.)?
a. 
⚠ NOTE: some user requests can be implicit (e.g., “Kenya” - implies that the user wants the assistant to tell them something about 
Kenya, or “a poem about raining” - implies that a user expects the assistant to write a poem about raining). 
5.Are there any links in the user request that I need to open to better understand the user request?
a. 
⚠ NOTE: Links should be treated as part of the user request. 
  
⚠ Paying attention to these details will help you evaluate whether the response follows the instructions in the user request.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Overview
Response Evaluation involves two steps: 
1.Single Response Rating. In this step, each response is analyzed against predefined criteria called dimensions and then rated to 
determine its overall quality. Dimensions outline the key factors or parameters that contribute to the overall quality or effectiveness of a 
response. 
2.Preference Ranking. In this step, the responses are compared and ranked against each other on a 7-point scale using the same 
dimensions. 
The following sections will provide guidance on selecting the appropriate ratings and ranking for each of these dimensions based on the 
Guidelines.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
2.1 Single Response Rating
Single response rating involves evaluating a particular response across the following dimensions: 
1.Following instructions 
2.Localization 
3.Concision 
4.Truthfulness 
5.Satisfaction 
Each dimension might have a set of dedicated criteria. Follow the detailed definitions of each criterion in the tabs below. 
⚠ NOTE: The way you evaluate Single Response Rating can vary depending on the assigned task. It’s important to note that not all tasks 
will include every parameter mentioned here. Each task may specifically ask for certain rating questions, but not necessarily all of them.
IMPORTANT UPDATE: Harmfulness-specific evaluation questions have been removed. However, if you encounter potentially harmful 
content, flag it in the comments, and refer to the Satisfaction section for updated guidance.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Following Instructions 
Review the user request. Make sure you fully understand the user request: 
1 - Conduct research if necessary to understand the user request and response. If the request contains links, open them as they are part of 
the user request.  
2 - Refer to the Attention to the Details section of the User Request analysis, to see if the response contains specific instructions for 
format, length, style, and particular actions.  
3 - Identify whether the instructions in the request are implicit or explicit.  
•Explicit Instructions: The instructions are clearly stated with details that do not leave room for interpretation of what the user request 
is asking. For example: 
•A user request explicitly (clearly) mentions the content, format, and length requirements, such as “give me 3 examples of major 
economic recessions in the history in bullet points sorted in reverse chronological order. Be sure to include the start and end year of 
each recession.” 
•A coding user request mentioned “just the code, no comment” ,  then the response should not contain comments. This is an explicit 
instruction.

2.1 Single Response Rating - Following Instructions

STEP 2. RESPONSE

•Following Instructions
•Localization
•Concision
•Truthfulness 
•Satisfaction
2.2. Preference Ranking 
•Satisfaction 
Aggregated Principles 
STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
•Implicit Instructions: The instructions can be inferred (understood), even if it is not clearly stated. 
•If the user request says “just the code” , implicit is that the response does not contain comments or descriptions about the code. If 
the user request was “how do I write some code to do…” , then it’s likely some description would enhance the helpfulness of the 
request and some commentary might be useful. 
•Questions & Answers are examples of implicit instructions. Asking questions is a form of instruction. In other words, the expectation 
is that the response contains the answer to the question. A similar line of reasoning applies to math/reasoning problems (which are 
often posed as questions). 
•Given that “Central America's oldest democratic republic which has had no standing army since 1948” there are no clear, explicit 
instructions in the user request but the implicit instruction is for the response to provide the correct name of the country that 
matches the information within the user request. 
•When context is present, you should also consider instructions implied by previous conversations. For example: 
-User: “Please rewrite this article in a way that a 5-year-old can understand. <text to write>” . 
- Assistant: “<Rewritten article>” . 
- User: “Another article please: <text to rewrite>” . In this example, it is implied that the user is seeking for rewriting the article in 
the same way as in their first request.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
IMPORTANT 
Following Instructions should not be mistaken for accuracy/truthfulness. An inaccurate response might still be following instructions and 
vise verse. For example: 
•The user asks to name ten countries starting with the letter ‘C’. The response contained 10 countries, meaning, it’s following the 
instructions even though some of the country names are made up! 
•For Question & Answers user requests, if the response is attempting to answer the question, it is following the instructions even if the 
answer is wrong.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Scale for Single Response Rating 
Scale Criteria
Fully 
following
•All explicit and implicit instructions are followed. 
•Declines to generate an inappropriate, offensive, or dangerous response with helpful disengagement. 
⚠ NOTE: Slight deviation in word count (5% above or beyond requested by the user) is concidered "Fully Following".
Not 
following
•Language: The user asks to provide the response in one language, but the assistant provides it in the other language.   
•The response fails to follow the main points of the explicit and implicit instructions. 
•Example: If 200 words are requested but a 500 word response is generated, this is "Not following" the instruction. Please note that a 
response that does not meet the word count requirement CANNOT be Very Satisfying. 
•
⚠ NOTE: The model might decline the user request due to the output limitation. For example, a user requests a 50,000-word essay. This 
is considered "Not following" instructions. However,  it's important to align the evaluation with users' expectations in the request.
Partially 
following
Most, but not all, of the explicit and implicit instructions are followed. The response generally follows the instructions, but there are some 
deviations. 
•Response Format: The user explicitly asks for a Yes/No answer, but the assistant added extra information in the response 
◦The user explicitly asks for a detailed response but the assistant defaults to Yes/No response.  
•Specific Details: The user explicitely specifies the length, style, format, etc. of the response and the assistant provides a response with 
deviations (two-paragraph response instead of requested one paragraph, summary of 3 sentences instead of requested 1 sentence, etc.)

⚠  NOTE: This dimension applies to all locales except en_US. If you are working on the en_US locale task, you will not see this dimension 
in the tool UI.

Aggregated Principles 
STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Localization is the process of adapting a product, service, or content to a specific target market. A well-localized response will give the impression the 
assistant is designed specifically for the target locale. T o achieve this, responses should:  
•Be written with native-level fluency, unless otherwise requested 
•Things such as spelling, vocabulary, and grammar should all be correct 
•Consider the context of the locale when providing information 
•It should provide the most relevant information for the target locale when possible 
•However, it should not be unnecessarily restricted answers about the locale (see "overly-localized content" below) 
•Use tone, phrases, and idioms that feel natural to a user in that locale 
•It should not overuse words, phrases, or idioms to the point of sounding like a stereotype 
•Use correct formatting for dates, addresses, etc, and correct units of measurement for distance, temperature, currency, etc. 
•Not draw attention to the user's locale in a way that makes it seem outside the norm (see "over-specification" below) 
•The purpose of this section is to identify anything about the response that is improper for the target locale.
You will be asked to answer the question "Are there any localization issues in the response?" using the scale below. 
Scale Description Comments
No issues The response shows no signs of being generated for a 
different locale than the target one.
If there are any issues with localization, this should 
not be selected.
Yes, issues 
present
There is at least one element of the response that would make 
the user question if the model were designed specifically for 
their locale
If there are any issues with localization, this must be 
selected.
Scale for Single Response Rating

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Issues Definition Examples
Unlocalized 
Information
The response provides 
information related to 
another locale when 
information more relevant to 
the target locale could have 
been provided instead.
Example 1: 
en_GB User Request: "What is the easiest way to file taxes?" 
Assistant Response: "Using IRS Free File. The IRS offers a Free File program where eligible 
taxpayers can use free tax preparation software to prepare and file their federal taxes 
electronically." 
Grade: Issues present, Unlocalized Information 
Explanation: The user is UK-based, but the IRS is a branch of the US government responsible for 
collecting taxes. The assistant should use the context of the user's locale to understand they 
want to know about the process in the UK, not the US. 
  
Example 2: 
en_CA User Request: When is the best time to visit Victoria?" 
Assistant Response: "Victoria, Australia, is a popular destination with diverse landscapes and a 
vibrant culture, making it a great place to visit throughout the year. However, the best time to visit 
Victoria depends on your personal preferences and the type of experience you want to have. 
Here is a breakdown of the different seasons in Victoria:" <list removed for brevity> 
Grade: Issues present, Unlocalized Information 
Explanation: Victoria, BC is the capital city of British Columbia, the 3rd largest province in 
Canada. The assistant should understand that based on the user's locale, this is the most 
relevant Victoria.
If Yes, issues present is selected, the follow-up Question will appear: Which issues are present? Select all that apply.

Issues Definition Examples
Overly-localized 
Content
The response unnecessarily fixates 
on the target locale. 
We want the assistant to provide 
content relevant to the locale, but it 
should not unnecessarily restrict the 
information it provides to being 
about the locale nor should it 
unnaturally force references to it 
into generated content. The 
assistant injects unnecessary locale 
specificity. 
Example: 
en_CA User Request: "What are the most influential books?" 
Assistant Response: "Canada has a rich literary tradition, and many Canadian authors 
have made significant contributions to literature. Here are some of the most influential 
books by Canadian authors: 
Classic Influential Books: 
1."Anne of Green Gables" by L.M. Montgomery (1908) 
2."The Handmaid's T ale" by Margaret Atwood (1985) 
3."Life of Pi" by Yann Martel (2001) 
4."The Stone Angel" by Margaret Laurence (1964) 
5."Fifth Business" by Robertson Davies (1970)" 
Grade: Issues present, Overly-localized content 
Explanation: This checkbox should be selected. The user did not request the list be only 
limited to Canadian authors, so the assistant should have considered books written by 
authors from all around the globe.

Issues Definition Examples
Spelling Any incorrect spelling, whether a 
typo, a non-standard spelling, a 
made up or gibberish word, must 
be marked as a localization issue.  
This applies to:  
•Any foreign words within the 
response that are considered 
acceptable in the locale 
•Any language or writing system 
(e.g, Chinese) 
Example: 
en_GB User Request: "what is your favourite colour?" 
Assistant Response: "As an AI, I don't have personal preferences, including favorite colors. 
However, I can tell you that blue is often considered a favorite color worldwide." 
Grade: Issues present, Spelling 
Explanation: The assistant used en_US spelling for "favourite" and “colours". 
en_GB User Request: "Can you explain how photosynthesis works?" 
Assistant Response: "Photosynthesis is the process by which plants convert sunglight 
into energy." 
Grade: Issues present, Spelling 
Explanation: "sunglight" is not a valid English word. 
Refer to the Locale Variant Guide for more examples.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Issues Definition Examples
Non-local 
Perspective 
(Formerly: 
Over-
specification)
The response is not 
from the 
perspective of the 
target locale. 
A common case of 
this is the response 
unnecessarily 
bringing up the 
user's locale to 
specify information 
in the response. 
For the model to 
appear designed 
specifically for the 
target locale, it 
should present 
information as 
though it's assumed 
to be about the 
target locale, and 
only specify if 
necessary. 
Example 1: 
en_AU User Request: "What is the most expensive housing market in the country?" 
Assistant Response: "According to the Real Estate Institute of Australia (REIA), Sydney, New South Wales, 
is currently the most expensive housing market in Australia. As of January 2023, the median house price in 
Sydney was AU$1.15 million." 
Grade: Issues present, Over-specification 
Explanation: For an Australian user, the dollar value should be assumed to be in Australian dollars without 
having to specify 
  
Example 2: 
en_AU User Request: "How many Australian dollars are in an American dollar?" 
Assistant Response: " 
As of April 11th, 2023 
•1 AUD is approximately 0.66 USD 
•1 USD is approximately 1.50 AUD 
Currency exchange rates fluctuate daily, even hourly, so it's recommended to check with a currency 
exchange service or bank for the most up-to-date rate before making any international transactions." 
Grade: No issues 
Explanation: In this case, it's necessary to specify which dollar belongs to which country, so the specification 
is not a localization issue.

Example 3: 
en_CA User Request: "What are some things to do on Victoria Day?" 
Assistant Response: "Victoria Day is a federal holiday in Canada that celebrates the birthday of Queen 
Victoria, the former monarch of the United Kingdom. It is observed on the Monday preceding May 25th each 
year. Here are some popular things to do on Victoria Day: 
<list removed for space>" 
Grade: Issues present, Over-specification 
Explanation: Victoria Day is a federal holiday that a model designed for the locale should treat as common 
knowledge. By defining it without the user requesting a definition, the model makes it seem foreign.

RESOURCES 
Issues Definition Examples
Units 
of measurement
The response uses incorrect units 
for measurements such as (but not 
limited to), unless specifically 
requested in another locale context: 
•Currency 
•Distance 
•Velocity 
•T emperature 
•Weight
Refer to the Locale Variant Guide for examples.
Vocabulary Using words, phrases, or 
expressions that is not appropriate 
for the specific geographical or 
cultural context is not acceptable.  
Example: 
en_GB User Request: "What are the most popular sports?” 
Assistant Response: "The United Kingdom has a rich sporting culture with a diverse 
range of popular games. According to a 2023 survey by Statista, the top 3 most popular 
sports in the UK based on participant interest are: 
1.Soccer 
2.Cricket 
3.Rugby Union” 
Grade: Issues present, Vocabulary 
Explanation: The assistant incorrectly used the word "soccer" when "football" should 
have been used. 
Refer to the Locale Variant Guide for more examples.

Issues Definition Examples
Phrase or IdiomThe response includes a phrase and 
idiom that is not commonly used or 
understood in the target locale.
Example: 
en_CA User Request: "Can you generate C++ code for me?" 
Assistant Response: "Yes, I'd be chuffed to bits to help you with C++ code! Please feel 
free to ask any questions, provide the code you'd like help with, or explain the problem 
you're trying to solve" 
Grade: Issues present, Phrase or idiom 
Explanation: "Chuffed to bits" is British colloquialism that would not be well understood by 
an en_CA user. 
Refer to the Locale Variant Guide for more examples.
Wrong 
Language  
The response contains foreign words 
that are not acceptable in the task 
locale (ranging from a single word to 
the full text). 
However: 
1.Responses that contain loanwords 
(borrowed or shared from other 
languages words) are acceptable if 
they are commonly used in the task 
locale.
It is unacceptable when a response is not in the same language as the request:  
User Request: iPhone15͸͍͘ΒͰ͔͢ (translation: how much is iPhone 15) 
Assistant Response: I'm sorry, your message is in a language that I cannot 
understand. This may be the result of an encoding error. 
User Request: 有关拜登减免学⽣债务的最新消息是什么？ 
Assistant Response: I am sorry, but I did not understand the request. Could you 
please rephrase your request in English?

Issues Definition Examples
Wrong 
Language  
1. Responses that contain coding 
languages are acceptable if the request 
is asking for a coding solution. 
2. Responses that contain foreign 
languages are acceptable if the intention 
of the request requires the response to 
be in a foreign language or its acceptable 
in the locale (e.g., requests to translate 
from a task locale to a foreign language, 
user requesting restaurant name 
recommendations or brand names).  
       Important Note on Wrong Language 
and Satisfaction Ratings 
Responses that contain one or a few 
foreign words that are not acceptable in the 
target locale should still be marked as a 
Localization > Wrong Language issue. 
However, the Satisfaction rating should be 
determined based on the severity of the 
foreign-language content. Use your best 
judgement.
Acceptable: 
User Request: how do I say "hello" in Mandarin? 
Assistant Response: 你好 (translation: hello) 
Use Request: Translate "how are you" to Spanish 
Assistant Response: "¿Cómo está usted?" 
Loanwords are acceptable:  
•Cul-de-sac (French): A dead-end street or road. 
•Fiancé(e) (French): A person engaged to be married. 
•Rendezvous (French): A meeting or appointment. 
•Entrepreneur (French): A person who starts a business or venture. 
•Angst (German): A feeling of deep anxiety or dread. 
•Kindergarten (German): A preschool for children aged three to five years. 
•Schnitzel (German): A breaded and fried meat cutlet, typically made with veal or pork. 
Coding languages: 
The following Python code response print ("Hello, World!") is acceptable. 
Refer to the Locale Variant Guide for more examples. 
ℹ

Issues Definition Examples
Wrong 
Language  •If the response is entirely or 
predominantly in the wrong language, it 
constitutes a critical failure and should 
receive a Highly Unsatisfying rating. 
•If the response is mostly in the correct 
language with limited language mixing, 
you should use your best judgment to 
select the appropriate Satisfaction rating. 
The evaluation tool now allows any 
Satisfaction rating to be selected after 
choosing Localization > Wrong Language, 
enabling more accurate reflection of issue 
severity.
Acceptable: 
User Request: how do I say “hello” in Mandarin? 
Assistant Response: 你好 (translation: hello) 
Use Request: Translate "How are you" to Spanish 
Assistant Response: "¿Cómo está usted?” 
Loanwords are acceptable:  
1.Cul-de-sac (French): A dead-end street or road. 
2.Fiancé(e) (French): A person engaged to be married. 
3.Rendezvous (French): A meeting or appointment. 
4.Entrepreneur (French): A person who starts a business or venture. 
5.Angst (German): A feeling of deep anxiety or dread. 
6.Kindergarten (German): A preschool for children aged three to five years. 
7.Schnitzel (German): A breaded and fried meat cutlet, typically made with veal or pork. 
Coding languages: 
The following Python code response print (“Hello, World!“) is acceptable. 
Refer to the Locale Variant Guide for more examples.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Issues Definition Examples
Grammar The response contains grammatical errors that do not 
conform to native usage in the target locale.  
Note: In earlier versions of the guidelines, grammatical errors 
were considered localization issues only if they were correct 
in another locale variant. This restriction no longer applies. 
Any grammar that is incorrect for the target locale should 
now be flagged as a localization issue.
learnt (UK) vs. learned (US) 
Refer to the Locale Variant Guide for examples. 
To n e Any tone that is inappropriate, unnatural, exaggerated, 
stereotypical, rude, dismissive, unprofessional, or otherwise 
unsuitable for the target locale should be marked as a 
localization issue. 
Note: All tone-related issues should now be evaluated under 
the Localization dimension. Characteristics such as formal 
vs. informal, serious vs. humorous, optimistic vs. cynical, or 
sympathetic vs. sarcastic should be flagged when they 
negatively affect appropriateness, clarity, or user experience. 
Refer to the T one examples in the Grading Examples document. 
Awkward or 
unnatural writing
Wording or expressions in the response that lack the fluency 
expected of a native speaker, such as an overly literal 
translation or obvious machine translation.
Refer to the Awkward or Unnatural Writing examples below.

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Issues Definition Examples
Formatting and 
Punctuation 
Select this category when the response contains any 
formatting or punctuation errors in the text. This includes (but 
is not limited to) incorrect date or time formats, improper or 
inconsistent punctuation, inappropriate quotation mark 
usage, incorrect spacing conventions, or incorrect text 
direction (e.g., left-to-right vs. right-to-left). 
This category applies only to text-level formatting and 
punctuations. It does not apply to content organization 
choices, such as whether information is presented in 
paragraphs, lists, tables, or other structural layouts. 
Note: In earlier version of the guidelines, formatting and 
punctuation errors were flagged as localization issues only 
when they conflicted with the rules of target locale. Under the 
current guidelines, this distinction no longer applies. Any 
formatting or punctuation error in the response, whether 
locale-specific or universally incorrect, should now be 
flagged under this category.
Refer to the Formatting and Punctuation 
examples in the Grading Examples document. 
Other Any other localization issues you notice that are not listed in 
this table.
Examples: 
Culturally insensitive content

User Request Assistant Response Issue Comment
What colour is the Golden 
Gate Bridge?
The Golden Gate Bridge is famously known for its 
color, which is not actually gold but instead an 
orange vermilion called "International Orange."
Yes The prompt used a variant of en_* such 
as en_AU/CA/GB where colour is spelled 
with a U, so the assistant responding 
with en_US spelling without the U could 
be considered a spelling mistake. 
However, we still expect the user would 
be able to read and fully understand this 
response.
什麼是恆星鏈，它有什麼新穎
之處？
The prompt is written in Mandarin. Would you like 
me to translate it?
Yes Responding in a language different from 
the prompt is only correct if the prompt 
requests it. In this case, it's the 
assistant's response suggesting a 
translation, not the prompt, and so it is 
not appropriate.

推荐三个可以喺 iPhone ⽤嘅⽽

我
It seems like you've shared a list of characters and 
symbols that don't form a coherent sentence or 
question in English. I'll do my best to understand 
and provide a response. 
It seems like you're looking for recommendations 
for popular news aggregation apps that you can 
use on your iPhone. Here are three popular options:

STEP 3. COMMENTS 
RESOURCES 
Examples:
Locale Example Localization 
Issues
Explanation
de_CH User Request: 
Schreibe einen Satz über einen lustigen Wochenendausflug. 
Assistant Response: 
Wir hatten an unserem Ausflug am Wochenende sehr viel Spaß. 
Spelling In the Swiss German locale (de_CH), the Eszett (ß) is 
not used. Words that are spelled with ß in other 
German locales (such as de_DE) should instead be 
written with “ss” in de_CH. The correct spelling in 
this locale is „Spass“ , not „Spaß“ . Using ß indicates a 
non-Swiss German spelling variant and therefore 
constitutes a localization spelling error.
de_DE User Request: 
Schreibe einen Satz über einen lustigen Wochenendausflug. 
Assistant Response: 
Wir hatten an unserem Ausflug am Wochenende sehr viel Spaß.
Spelling In the German (Germany) locale (de_DE), the word 
Spaß is spelled using the Eszett (ß). The assistant’s 
spelling aligns with standard orthographic 
conventions for this locale and does not indicate 
influence from another German variant. Therefore, 
no localization spelling issue is present.
de_DE Er lehrte mich nicht nur Englisch, sondern auch, wie man kritisch denkt, 
vernünftig kommuniziert und meine Gedanken und Gefühle ausdrückt. 
Response: 
Mr. Johnson, mein Highschool-Englischlehrer, hatte einen bleibenden 
Einfluss auf mein Leben. Er war mehr als nur ein Lehrer; er war ein 
Mentor und Inspirator, der mich ermutigte, mein Potenzial 
auszuschöpfen und härter zu arbeiten. 
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
The last part of the sentence sounds like a one to 
one translation. The correct expression would be 
"und man seine Gedanken und Gefühle ausdrücken 
kann".

Seine Begeisterung für Literatur war ansteckend, und er vermittelte 
jedes Thema mit echter Leidenschaft. Er ermutigte uns, über die T exte 
hinaus zu denken und ihre tieferen Bedeutungen zu erforschen, was 
meine Liebe zum Lesen und Schreiben entfachte. Er lehrte mich nicht 
nur Englisch, sondern auch, wie man kritisch denkt, vernünftig 
kommuniziert und meine Gedanken und Gefühle ausdrückt. 
Eines der bemerkenswertesten Dinge an Mr. Johnson war seine 
Fähigkeit, das Potenzial in jedem Schüler zu erkennen. Er glaubte an uns, 
auch wenn wir selbst nicht an uns glaubten. Er erinnerte mich ständig 
daran, dass ich die Fähigkeit hatte, große Dinge zu erreichen, wenn ich 
bereit war, mir die Mühe zu machen. Sein Vertrauen in mich gab mir den 
Selbstvertrauen, Herausforderungen anzunehmen und mich außerhalb 
meiner Komfortzone zu wagen. 
Mr. Johnson lehrte mich auch wichtige Lebenslektionen über Disziplin, 
Hingabe und harte Arbeit. Er erwartete von uns das Beste und 
akzeptierte nichts weniger. Seine Anforderungen zwangen mich, mich 
anzustrengen und mein volles Potenzial auszuschöpfen, was mir bei 
allen meinen zukünftigen Unternehmungen zugute kam. Ich bin ihm für 
die Hingabe und das Engagement dankbar, die er mir beigebracht hat, 
da sie mich zu dem Menschen gemacht haben, der ich heute bin. 
Seine Mentorschaft ging über den Klassenzimmer hinaus. Er machte 
sich Zeit für uns, bot Ratschläge und war ein geduldiger Zuhörer. Er 
kümmerte sich wirklich um das Wohlbefinden und die Zukunft seiner 
Schüler. Er hatte einen bleibenden Einfluss auf mein Leben, und ich bin 
ihm für seine unerschütterliche Unterstützung und seinen Glauben an 
mich dankbar.

Mr. Johnson, mein Highschool-Englischlehrer, hatte einen bleibenden

Mentor und Inspirator, der mich ermutigte, mein Potenzial

Unnatural 
Writing

Zeit für uns".

de_DE Ich hoffe, diese Nachricht findet dich gut und in guter Stimmung, 
Response: 
Hallo T ommy, 
Ich hoffe, diese Nachricht findet dich gut und in guter Stimmung, 
während du deine unglaubliche Reise durch Italien startest. Als dein 
Vater kann ich nicht anders, als ein Gefühl der Stolz und Freude zu 
haben, dass du diese wunderbare Gelegenheit ergreifst, deine 
kulturellen Kenntnisse zu erweitern und etwas Italienisch zu lernen. 
Italien ist ein Land mit einer reichen Geschichte, Kunst, Musik und 
natürlich einer köstlichen Küche. Machen Sie sich bereit, sich auf eine 
kulinarische Reise zu begeben, die Sie nie vergessen werden. Probieren 
Sie so viele verschiedene Gerichte wie möglich aus, von köstlichem 
Pasta bis hin zu schmelzendem Gelato. Vergessen Sie nicht, die 
regionalen Spezialitäten zu genießen, denn jeder T eil Italiens hat sein 
eigenes kulinarisches Schatz. 
Denken Sie daran, dass das Erlernen einer neuen Sprache eine 
Herausforderung sein kann, aber es ist auch eine unglaublich lohnende 
Erfahrung. T auchen Sie ein in die italienische Kultur, sprechen Sie mit 
den Einheimischen und üben Sie so viel wie möglich. Ich bin sicher, dass 
Sie Ihre Sprachkenntnisse schnell verbessern und neue Freunde auf 
dieser Reise finden werden.
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
This response sound unnatural and awkward, like a 
word by word translation.

de_DE Sie könnten Ihre Reise in Florenz beginnen, der Hauptstadt der T oskana 
und Heimat vieler kultureller Schätze wie den Dom, die Uffizien und die 
Ponte Vecchio. Von dort aus könntest du T agesausflüge in Städte wie 
Siena, Pisa, Lucca und San Gimignano machen, die alle innerhalb einer 
Stunde mit dem Zug oder Auto erreichbar sind. 
Response: 
Sicher! Eine großartige Region in Italien, die perfekt für eine Woche voller 
Städtetrips ist, ist die T oskana. Die T oskana ist bekannt für ihre 
atemberaubende Landschaft, ihre historischen Städte und ihre 
exzellente Küche. 
Sie könnten Ihre Reise in Florenz beginnen, der Hauptstadt der T oskana 
und Heimat vieler kultureller Schätze wie den Dom, die Uffizien und die 
Ponte Vecchio. Von dort aus könntest du T agesausflüge in Städte wie 
Siena, Pisa, Lucca und San Gimignano machen, die alle innerhalb einer 
Stunde mit dem Zug oder Auto erreichbar sind. 
Jede dieser Städte hat ihren eigenen einzigartigen Charme und bietet 
eine Fülle von Sehenswürdigkeiten, Kunst, Architektur und Gastronomie. 
Du könntest auch die malerische Landschaft der T oskana erkunden, 
Weinproben machen und die Ruhe und Schönheit der Region genießen. 
Die T oskana ist eine perfekte Region für eine Woche voller Städtetrips, 
ohne zu weit fahren zu müssen, und bietet eine unvergessliche 
Reiseerfahrung. Viel Spaß bei deiner Reise!
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
Inconsistent use of personal pronouns. In the first 
sentence the formal pronoun "Sie" is used, whereas 
in the second sentence uses the informal "du".

de_DE Kannst du mir erklären, was künstliche Intelligenz ist? 
Response: 
"Künstliche Intelligenz ist ein Bereich der Informatik, der sich mit der 
Entwicklung von Systemen beschäftigt, die menschenähnliche 
Fähigkeiten zeigen."
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
In the German locale, quotation marks are written as 
„Example T ext“ rather than "Example T ext” . 
(Wikipedia)
de_DE User Request:
Wann findet das nächste T eammeeting statt? 
Assistant Response:
Das nächste T eammeeting findet am 04/15/2024 um 3:00 PM statt.
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
In German (de_DE), dates in everyday usage follow a 
day–month–year order and are typically written with 
periods as separators (e.g., 15.04.2024). Time is 
usually expressed using the 24-hour clock (e.g., 
15:00). The assistant instead used a month–day–
year format with slashes (04/15/2024) and a 12-
hour clock with AM/PM, both of which are 
inconsistent with standard German conventions 
outside of technical or administrative contexts. This 
formatting would appear foreign or unlocalized to a 
German user.

de_DE Sehr geehrter Herr Müller,
vielen Dank für Ihre Nachricht und die übersandten Unterlagen. Ich habe 
sie mir bereits angesehen und werde Ihnen bis Ende der Woche eine 
Rückmeldung geben.
Falls du in der Zwischenzeit noch Fragen hast, kannst du dich jederzeit 
melden. Ich denke, Sie werden mit dem Ergebnis zufrieden sein.
Mit freundlichen Grüßen
Anna Schmidt
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
The text illustrates an unintended shift between 
formal and informal forms of address in German. It 
begins with the formal pronoun Sie and 
corresponding possessive forms (Ihre, Ihnen), which 
are appropriate in a professional or polite context. 
Midway through the message, the writer switches to 
the informal du forms (du, dich), before returning to 
Sie. 
This inconsistency violates German norms of 
address, where maintaining a single level of formality 
is expected within the same interaction unless an 
explicit change has been agreed upon. Such shifts 
are often perceived as unprofessional or socially 
awkward.
de_DE Incorrect gender - 'DAS Kauf eines Hauses' , correct is 'DER Kauf eines 
Hauses';
Incorrect casing - 'ich möchte DEM Haus kaufen', correct is 'ich möchte 
DAS Haus kaufen'
Grammar Nouns have genders and cases which can change 
depending on context. Incorrect casing - 'ich 
möchte DEM Haus kaufen', correct is 'ich möchte 
DAS Haus kaufen'

de_DE / 
de_AT
Am Samstag ging er früh auf den Markt, um frische Erdäpfel und süße 
Marillen zu kaufen. Die Bäuerin empfahl ihm außerdem etwas Topfen 
und ein Stück Käse, perfekt für eine Jause im Garten. Später saß er 
gemütlich auf dem Bankerl, trank einen Verlängerten und genoß das 
schöne Wetter. Es war ein ganz normaler, aber richtig schöner T ag.
VocabularyThis text uses Austrian German vocabulary. Words 
like “Erdäpfel” mean potatoes (instead of the 
standard German 'Kartoffeln'), and 'Marillen' means 
apricots (standard German: 'Aprikosen'). 
Other terms such as 'Jause' (a snack), 'T opfen' 
(quark/curd cheese), and 'Verlängerter' (a type of 
coffee) are commonly used in Austria and reflect 
everyday regional language.

en_GB Prompt: Why is T aylor Swift recording all her albums again?

them. This allows her to regain control over her music catalogue, and 
ensure that every penny made from her old hits goes straight to her 
pocket, rather than to folk she reckons didn't earnt it.

control she had over her creative legacy. She expressed dismay at the 
prospect of her old songs being used without her consent or knowledge, 
potentially undermining the integrity of her artistic vision. This 
apprehension was fueled when Big Machine Records was sold to 
Scooter Braun, a prominent music manager who had previously been 
involved in public feuds with Swift.

en_IN User Request
Can you help me write a professional email?” 
Response 
Howdy! I’d be happy to help you draft that email.” 
Phrase or 
idiom 
“Howdy” is an American regional greeting and is 
not commonly used or understood in Indian 
English. A neutral greeting such as “Hello” would 
be more appropriate for the Indian locale. 
Greetings such as G'day Mate, Howdy are not 
en_IN specific and needs to be marked down

en_IN User Request:

Assistant Response: 
“Cloud computing, per se, internet ke through services provide karne 
ka model hai.” 
Wrong 
Language 
The assistant response has a different script 
showing up "Hinglish" so we mark it down as 
Wrong Language 
es_** Response: No dudes en hablar con tus amigos, familiares o un 
consejero si te sientes abrumado. Compartir tus sentimientos puede 
ayudarte a lighten the load and obtener nuevas perspectivas. No tienes 
que lidiar con esto solo; buscar apoyo es un signo de fortaleza, no de 
debilidad. 
Correct version: No dudes en hablar con tus amigos, familiares o un 
consejero si te sientes abrumado. Compartir tus sentimientos puede 
ayudarte a aligerar la carga y obtener nuevas perspectivas. No tienes 
que lidiar con esto solo; buscar apoyo es un signo de fortaleza, no de 
debilidad.
Wrong 
Language
"lighten the load and" is in wrong language.  
Note: Even if there is a single word in the response 
that is in an incorrect language. It should be marked 
under Localization - wrong language.

es_ES Aquí está una explicación 
Response: 
"Claro, aquí está una explicación que le puedo dar a un niño de primaria 
sobre los resfriados: 
Un resfriado es una enfermedad que puede causarte sentir mal y tener 
algunos síntomas como tos, dolor de garganta y secreción nasal. Los 
resfriados son muy comunes y suelen desaparecer solos después de 
una semana o así. Sin embargo, algunos síntomas como el dolor de 
garganta o la congestión nasal pueden durar un poco más. La tos 
también puede durar algunas semanas más, pero eventualmente 
desaparecerá. 
Lo importante es descansar mucho y tomar suficiente líquido para 
sentirte mejor. Si tienes fiebre o te sientes muy mal, es posible que 
necesites hablar con un médico para que te dé algo para sentirte mejor."
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
Overall, the text has an unnatural tone. There are 
several sentences that were translated literally and 
sound awkward, such as: 
- Aquí está una explicación: We would use the verb 
tener, "aquí tienes una explicación". 
- Causarte sentir mal: Since the text is about health, 
we would say "causar malestar" or "hacer que te 
encuentres mal". 
- Después de una semana o así: It should be "al 
cabo de una semana más menos" because "o así" is 
a literal translation of "or so". 
Furthermore, the assistant uses the verb “sentir” too 
many times instead of looking for synonyms, which 
impacts the fluency of the text.

es_ES Darle más pensamiento 
Response: 
Querida [Nombre de la esposa], 
Espero que este mensaje te encuentre bien. Me gustaría compartir 
algunas actualizaciones sobre mis planes para el viaje. Después de darle 
más pensamiento, he decidido pasar otra noche en Boston con mis 
padres. La salud de mi padre ha estado empeorando rápidamente, y 
siento que necesito aprovechar al máximo cada momento con ellos, no 
sé cuándo volveré a verlos. 
Sé que esto significa cambiar nuestros planes, y lo siento mucho por 
eso. Espero que puedas entender la importancia de esto para mí. T e 
aseguro que haré todo lo posible para volver a Los Ángeles lo antes 
posible y ponerme al día con todo lo que he perdido. 
Por favor, sabes que te echo de menos mucho. No puedo evitar pensar 
en ti y en lo mucho que me gustaría que estuvieras aquí conmigo. Estoy 
haciendo todo lo posible para manejar esto, y pronto estaremos juntos 
de nuevo. 
Por favor, cuídate mucho y mantenme informado de todo lo que está 
sucediendo allí. T e quiero mucho y espero hablar contigo pronto. 
Con amor, 
[Tu nombre]
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
The text sounds very awkward due to literal 
translations from English, including: 
- Espero que este mensaje te encuentre bien: Native 
speakers don't use this greeting. 
- Darle más pensamiento: We would say "pensarlo 
más". 
- Lo siento mucho por eso: "Por eso" should be 
omitted. 
- T e echo de menos mucho: The correct order is "te 
echo mucho de menos". 
- Con amor: We would sign off with "con cariño" or 
"un abrazo" instead.

es_ES Los objetos geométricos (...) pueden ser deformados y flexionados 
Response:  
"En este mundo alternativo, introduciría un nuevo axioma llamado "Axioma 
de la flexibilidad". Este axioma establece que todos los objetos 
geométricos, incluyendo líneas, planos y figuras, pueden ser deformados 
y flexionados de manera plástica sin romperse ni desaparecer. 
Este axioma permitiría la creación de formas y estructuras geométricas 
únicas y sorprendentes, ya que los objetos podrían ser doblados, 
estirados y torcidos de maneras que no se pueden hacer en el mundo 
real. Los edificios podrían tener formas fluidas y cambiantes, los objetos 
podrían ser diseñados para transformarse de una forma a otra, y las líneas 
y planos podrían ser flexionados para crear patrones intrincados y 
complejos. 
Además, este nuevo axioma también tendría implicaciones en la física y la 
mecánica del mundo, ya que las propiedades de los materiales y los 
objetos podrían ser alteradas por su capacidad para ser deformados y 
flexionados. Esto podría llevar a la creación de nuevos materiales con 
propiedades únicas, nuevos métodos de construcción y nuevas formas 
de moverse y interactuar con el entorno. 
En resumen, el Axioma de la flexibilidad introduciría una nueva dimensión 
a la geometría, permitiendo la creación de formas y estructuras únicas y 
abriendo nuevas posibilidades en la física y la mecánica del mundo."
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
Overuse of passive sentences, which are quite 
common in English, but in Spanish it makes the text 
harder to read because it's unnatural. It would be 
smoother with a reflexive pronoun: "los objetos (...) 
se pueden deformar y flexionar".

es_ES "Esoterismo eslavo" o "Rodnoverie" (...) "upyrs" 
Response: 
"La teoría de conspiración que usted mencionó parece estar relacionada 
con un movimiento conocido como "Esoterismo eslavo" o "Rodnoverie" 
(Nativo de la fe), que es una forma de neopaganismo que se ha 
desarrollado en Rusia y otros países de la antigua Unión Soviética. Este 
movimiento busca revivir y reconstruir las antiguas creencias y prácticas 
paganas de los pueblos eslavos antes de su conversión al cristianismo. 
Según algunas de las creencias dentro del Rodnoverie, los antiguos 
eslavos adoraban a una serie de dioses y diosas, y creían en una fuerza 
cósmica llamada "Rod" que gobernaba el universo. T ambién creían que 
existían seres malignos, como los "upyrs" o vampiros, y los "lichy", que 
eran criaturas similares a los lagartos que eran vistos como enemigos de 
los eslavos. 
En la teoría de conspiración que su estudiante le mencionó, parece que 
se combinan estos elementos para crear una historia en la que los rusos 
lucharon contra una raza de personas lagarto que intentaban tomar sus 
tierras. Se dice que los rusos bebieron del lago Baikal, que les otorgó 
poderes de los antiguos dioses eslavos, y que así fueron capaces de 
derrotar a estas criaturas y proteger su tierra. 
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
The assistant always uses English quotation marks 
( " " ). The correct quotation marks in Spanish are 
the guillemets ( « » ). English quotation marks 
should only be used inside guillemets.

es_ES Response:
El ballet contemporáneo se ha ido abriendo paso a lo largo de las últimas 
décadas.
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
The word ballet is a extranjerismo crudo: a foreign 
word that hasn't been adapted formally into Spanish, 
and it's included in the DLE as is. Extranjerismos 
crudos should be written in cursive as per grammar. 
This should be flagged as a formatting issue.
es_ES Response:

Muchas gracias por haberte tomado la molestia de escribirme. ¡Espero 
que podamos vernos pronto!
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
There are two punctuation issues in this response:
1. There is a comma missing between "Hola" and 
"Rosendo".
2. In Spanish, greetings in letters or emails should be 
followed by a colon instead of a comma.

es_ES Response: 
Aquí tienes algunos platos con arroz que te pueden gustar: 
- Arroz a Banda: Este es un plato típico de la gastronomía valenciana.

"anduvo". Although used frequently, the forms "andó", 
"andé" and similar are not grammatically correct.

Punctuation

es_ES Response: 
El 46% de 98.523 es 45.320.
Formatting and 
Punctuation
In Spanish, the number and the percentage symbol 
should be separated with a space (46 %). 
Furthermore, we shouldn't use a period nor a comma 
to separate thousands, we should use a space (98 
523).
es_ES Response: 
Me encantaría ayudarte con tu proyecto de investigación. Por favor 
proporcione los datos.
Tone In Spanish, whenever you are referring to someone 
there's the "tú" verb form, which is more casual, and 
the "usted" form, which is more formal, but mixing 
both is not correct. It should be one or the other, 
depending on the situation.

fr_FR C'était son premier album de nouveaux matériaux depuis son album "Evermore". 
Response: 
Voici quelques informations sur la sortie de l'album "Midnights" de T aylor Swift 
en 2022 : 
T aylor Swift a sorti son dixième album studio, "Midnights", le 21 octobre 2022. 
C'était son premier album de nouveaux matériaux depuis son album "Evermore" 
sorti en décembre 2020. L'annonce de l'album a eu lieu lors de la cérémonie des 
MTV Video Music Awards le 28 août 2022, où Swift a remporté le prix du 
meilleur clip vidéo pour "All T oo Well : The Short Film". 
Swift a décrit "Midnights" comme un album de chansons sur "les nuits sans 
sommeil" et des histoires qu'elle a ressassées "à 3 heures du matin". L'album a 
un son pop, électronique et expérimental, avec des influences de synth-pop et 
de dream pop. Les paroles de l'album traitent de sujets tels que l'amour, la 
nostalgie, l'anxiété et la célébrité. 
L'album "Midnights" a reçu des critiques positives de la part des critiques 
musicaux, qui ont loué la production, les paroles et la voix de Swift. L'album a 
également connu un succès commercial important, battant plusieurs records de 
streaming et de vente en une semaine. Par exemple, il a été l'album le plus vendu 
aux États-Unis en une semaine depuis "Reputation" de Swift en 2017, et il a 
battu le record du plus grand nombre d'écoutes en une journée sur Spotify. 
En résumé, la sortie de l'album "Midnights" de T aylor Swift en 2022 a été un 
événement majeur dans l'industrie de la musique, avec des critiques positives et 
un succès commercial important.
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
- "de nouveaux matériaux" seems like a litteral 
translation of the English "new materials", which 
would not work in French
- The expression "premier album de nouveaux 
matériaux" is also awkward. A more natural 
structure may be to replace the preposition "de" 
with a gerondive.
- French speakers are very sensitive to 
repetitions of words and try to avoid them as 
much as possible in writing. Here, "album" is 
used twice in the sentence. A synonym could 
have been found for one of the occurences. 
- sentence starting by "Swift": we would use the 
entire name of the artist.

fr_FR Oui, j'ai fait. 
Response: 
CARACTÈRE 1 : CLIENT (C) 
CARACTÈRE 2 : CONSEILLER FINANCIER (F) 
(SCÈNE : UN BUREAU DANS UNE COMPAGNIE D'INVESTISSEMENT. LE 
CLIENT EST ASSIS DEVANT LE BUREAU DU CONSEILLER FINANCIER, 
QUI EST ASSIS SUR LE BUREAU.) 
F : Bonjour, monsieur Johnson. Merci d'être venu me voir aujourd'hui. 
C : Pas de problème, monsieur Smith. J'étais ravi de recevoir votre appel. 
F : Alors, commençons par jeter un coup d'œil à vos dossiers financiers. 
J'ai vu que vous avez investi une partie considérable de votre argent 
dans des actifs à haut risque. 
C : Oui, j'ai fait. J'ai entendu dire qu'ils peuvent offrir de hauts 
rendements. 
F : Bien sûr, mais ils sont également accompagnés de plus grands 
risques. Et en regardant vos dépenses, je dois vous mettre en garde 
contre toute implication future dans de tels actifs. 
C : Cela semble fantastique. Faisons-le. 
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
t seems like a litteral translation of English "yes, I 
did". In French, the verb "faire" is transitive so it 
needs to be followed by an object. It is also never 
used in a short expression to say "yes" or "no" like in 
English. 
- "une partie considérable de votre argent" : 
grammatically correct but sounds a bit dramatic and 
translated. "vous avez réalisé des investissements 
importants etc."
- "Oui, j'ai fait. J'ai entendu dire qu'ils peuvent offrir 
de hauts rendements." - see comment made 
previously on "yes I did", and "hauts 
rendements"should be changed to "rendements 
importants". Although it is grammatically correct and 
understandable, does not sounds natural. 
“mais votre mode de vie actuel montre le contraire" 
sounds unnatural, we would go for something like 
"mais votre train de vie indique le contraire” 
- "Je vois ce que vous dites" is unnatural , we will 
phrase it differently: "je comprends ce que vous 
voulez dire" or "je vois ce que vous voulez dire” .

Locale Example Localization 
Issues Explanation
C : Pourquoi cela ? Je peux gérer le risque. 
F : Je comprends, monsieur Johnson, mais votre mode de vie actuel 
montre le contraire. Si vous continuez à investir dans ces actifs et que 
les choses tournent mal, vous pourriez tout perdre. 
C : Je vois ce que vous dites. Alors, que me suggérez-vous ? 
F : Je pense que vous devriez vous concentrer sur des investissements 
plus sûrs, comme des actions ou des obligations. Ils peuvent offrir un 
rendement modéré, mais ils sont moins risqués. 
C : D'accord, je pense que c'est une bonne idée. J'aimerais vous 
entendre parler de quelques options. 
F : Bien sûr, je serais ravi de vous aider. Nous pouvons examiner vos 
objectifs financiers et créer un portefeuille d'investissement 
personnalisé qui correspond à vos besoins. 
C : Cela semble fantastique. Faisons-le. 
F : Merci, monsieur Johnson. Je suis heureux de pouvoir vous aider. 
(LA SCÈNE SE TERMINE.)
- "J'aimerais vous entendre parler de quelques 
options" does not sound natural, we would go for 
"J'aimerais en savoir plus sur les options/ solutions/ 
produits que vous pourriez proposer."
- "Cela semble fantastique: Faisons-le" is also a 
litteral translation : in french it would rather be "c'est 
parfait, faisons comme ça"
- "Merci, monsieur Johnson. Je suis heureux de 
pouvoir vous aider" would be phrased differently as 
this is a littéral translation of english manner. be "très 
bien, je suis ravi de pouvoir vous aider."

fr_FR "Maintenant, j'aimerais en savoir plus sur votre amour pour le paiement de la 
pension alimentaire." 
Response: 
[Musique d'introduction] 
[Hôte] : Bienvenue dans "Interesting Legal Cases", où nous explorons des 
cas juridiques uniques et intrigants qui ont remodelé le paysage juridique. Je 
m'appelle votre hôte, [Nom de l'hôte], et dans cet épisode, nous allons nous 
lancer dans un voyage au cœur d'une question passionnante : l'amour, le 
mariage et les obligations alimentaires. Rejoignez-nous alors que nous 
accueillons Alimony T ony, un homme indépendant qui embrasse la romance 
avec un flair unique. 
[Musique et effets sonores] 
[Hôte] : Alimony T ony, merci d'être avec nous sur le podcast aujourd'hui. 
[Alimony T ony] : Merci d'avoir invité, c'est un plaisir d'être ici. 
[Hôte] : Alors, pour commencer, peux-tu nous parler de ta passion pour le 
mariage ? 
[Alimony T ony] : Bien sûr ! Je suis un homme romantique, et je crois 
vraiment en la magie du mariage. Quand je tombe amoureux, je suis tout 
entier dans la relation. Je pense que le mariage est un moyen de s'engager 
et de s'unir avec quelqu'un que vous aimez.
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
- The sentence uses rather generic words (amour, 
paiement), without a precise semantic meaning. This 
makes it sound like it was not written by a very 
competent writer. 
- The use of the article "la" and of the singular is 
clumsy, it would have been better to use an 
inderminate article ("des") and make it plural. 
- The structure of the sentence does not flow well. A 
human translator would have likely rephrased/
reorganised the sentence completely to make it 
sound more natural. 
- “Merci d'avoir invité" - this sentence in French 
does not make sense as we would need to add an 
object to the verb (e.g. de m'avoir invité) or change it 
to a noun (e.g. merci pour l’invitation) 
- "peux-tu nous parler..." "j'aimerais en savoir plus 
sur votre amour pour..." "C'est très généreux de ta 
part. Vous avez actuellement" - the whole 
interaction changes from the informal form of "you" 
to the formal version (between the same speakers). 
We need alignment as it sounds unnatural. Either 
they address each other in one form or the other, but 
they would not switch from one sentence to another

[Hôte] : C'est beau. Maintenant, j'aimerais en savoir plus sur votre amour 
pour le paiement de la pension alimentaire. Qu'est-ce qui vous a amené 
à prendre cette décision ? 
[Alimony T ony] : Eh bien, je crois que quand deux personnes se marient, 
elles s'engagent à prendre soin l'une de l'autre. Même si la relation se 
termine, je crois toujours qu'il est important de fournir un soutien à l'ex-
partenaire. C'est ma façon de montrer que je tiens à eux et que je veux 
leur bien-être. 
[Hôte] : C'est très généreux de ta part. Vous avez actuellement sept ex-
femmes, et vous leur donnez chacune 12 000 $ par mois. Cela 
représente une grande partie de votre revenu. Comment gérez-vous vos 
finances pour vous permettre de faire cela ? 
[Alimony T ony] : C'est vrai, le paiement de la pension alimentaire est une 
priorité pour moi, et je l'organise dans mes finances. J'ai hérité d'une 
entreprise prospère de ma mère, qui fabrique du papier gazeux, ce qui 
me permet de subvenir à mes besoins et à ceux de mes ex-femmes. Je 
considère cela comme un investissement dans le bonheur et le bien-
être d'eux. 
[Hôte] : C'est admirable. Cela a dû être une décision difficile de prendre, 
surtout compte tenu du fait que cela pourrait avoir un impact sur vos 
finances personnelles. Avez-vous jamais regretté de payer une pension 
alimentaire à vos ex-femmes ?
"le bonheur et le bien-être d'eux" - we would not 
phrase it like that, but we would say "leur bien-être"
- "Cela a dû être une décision difficile de prendre" - 
we would say "à prendre"
- "Je comprends où ils viennent" - the expression in 
French is "je comprends d'où ils viennent"
- "Je m'appelle votre hôte" : the presenter will not 
present his function. They present rather the 
invitees. 
- "un homme indépendant qui embrasse la romance 
avec un flair unique." This is a litteral translation. we 
would use another verb as "embrasser" has a 
different meaning and use.
- "Quand je tombe amoureux, je suis tout entier dans 
la relation." we would translate differently ( je 
m'investis pleinement etc)
- "je l'organise dans mes finances." would have 
been translated differently : "c'est un budget à part 
entière". or something like that.

fr_FR Slide 3: Amélioration de la santé cardiovasculaire 
Response: 
Malheureusement, je ne peux pas créer une présentation directement 
dans Google Slides. Cependant, voici un exemple de plan que vous 
pourriez suivre pour créer votre propre présentation sur les bienfaits de 
l'activité physique régulière sur la santé : 
Slide 1: Titre - Les bienfaits de l'activité physique régulière sur la santé 
Slide 2: Introduction - Définition de l'activité physique régulière et son 
importance pour la santé 
Slide 3: Amélioration de la santé cardiovasculaire 
Slide 4: Renforcement des muscles et des os 
Slide 5: Contrôle du poids 
Slide 6: Réduction du risque de maladies chroniques (diabète, cancer, 
etc.) 
Slide 7: Amélioration de la santé mentale et du bien-être 
Slide 8: Recommandations pour une pratique sportive régulière 
Slide 9: Conclusion - Résumé des bienfaits de l'activité physique 
régulière sur la santé 
N'oubliez pas d'ajouter des images, des graphiques et des chiffres pour 
illustrer les différents points et rendre votre présentation plus 
dynamique. Bonne création !
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
This is not a big issue as French is not extremely 
strict with typographical variants. However, the 
correct typography for French formatting would be 
to add spacings on both sides of a colon.

fr_FR C'est comme un jeu de mots dans lequel "la précision" et "la brièveté" sont 
vos meilleurs amis. 
Response: 
Titre : "The (Not So) Secret Diary of a New Lawyer : 10 Things I Wish I Knew 
Before Becoming a Lawyer" 
Cher lecteur, 
Bienvenue dans le monde trépidant de l'avocature ! Où les rêves de porter 
des costumes à trois pièces et de défendre la justice dans des salles 
d'audience comme Perry Mason se heurtent rapidement à la réalité de la 
rédaction de documents juridiques et de la recherche de documents dans 
des fichiers de cour. En tant que diplômé récent de la faculté de droit qui a 
traversé le feu, je suis là pour partager les 10 choses que j'aurais aimé savoir 
avant de devenir avocat. Alors, attachez vos ceintures de sécurité, et 
préparez-vous pour un voyage hilarant et éclairant. 
1 Le talent de la rédaction est un super pouvoir : 
Vous avez peut-être excellé dans vos cours de droit, mais laissez-moi vous 
dire que la rédaction de documents juridiques est une compétence tout à fait 
différente. C'est comme un jeu de mots dans lequel "la précision" et "la 
brièveté" sont vos meilleurs amis. Préparez-vous à transformer vos phrases 
complexes en paragraphes clairs et concis qui pourraient endormir même le 
professeur le plus agité. Conseil : pratiquez la rédaction de notes pour vous-
même comme si vous étiez un blogueur juridique, et vous serez un maître de 
la clarté en aucun temps.
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
The assistant always uses English quotation marks ( 
" " ). Like Spanish, the traditional quotation marks in 
French are the guillemets ( « » ). However note that 
other types of quotations marks, including English 
quotations, may be used from time to time, 
depending on the typography policy of each 
organisation and the style the writer wants to give 
to its document. 
- for each paragraph number, we would add a bullet 
point after each number (e.g. "1. Le talent de la 
rédaction est un super pouvoir :")

2 La recherche de documents est comme une chasse au trésor : 
Imaginez cela : des piles de documents, des fichiers numériques et des 
années de procès à fouiller. La recherche de documents est l'aventure 
d'un avocat, et la découverte de la pièce maîtresse du puzzle peut être 
aussi gratifiante que de trouver la dernière figurine Funko. Conseil : 
développez votre amour pour les mots-clés et ne soyez pas intimidé par 
les piles de documents. Vous serez un détective de documents en un 
rien de temps. 
3 L'art de la négociation est une danse comme aucune autre : 
La négociation est plus qu'une simple transaction ; c'est une danse de la 
persuasion. Vous devez être capable de lire entre les lignes, de 
comprendre les besoins de votre client et de trouver un terrain 
d'entente. C'est comme une partie de poker où votre visage impassible 
est votre atout. Conseil : écoutez attentivement, soyez empathique et ne 
soyez pas intimidé par les échanges de piques. Vous apprendrez bientôt 
à naviguer dans les négociations comme un pro. 
4 La patience est une vertu : 
Le système judiciaire peut être aussi lent qu'une tortue en hiver, et 
parfois, il semble que la justice ne soit pas à portée de main. Apprenez à 
être patient et à faire confiance au processus. C'est comme attendre 
que votre commande de café prenne bien trop longtemps, mais au 
moins, vous n'avez pas à vous soucier que quelqu'un soit accusé à tort. 
Conseil : adoptez un mantra de patience et faites des activités 
relaxantes pour vous aider à rester calme. La méditation ou le cri 
peuvent fonctionner.

hi_IN Example 1 
User Request 
िहन्दी में 'Special' और 'Life' के िलए शब्द बताए ं । 
(Give the words for 'Special' and 'Life' in Hindi.) 
Response: यह बहुत ही खास बात है िक आप अपनी िजंदगी में सफल हो रहे हैं। 
(It is very important that you are succeeding in your life.) 
Example 2 
User Request: 
नया काम शुरू करने के िलए िनदेर्श दें। 
(Give instructions to start a new job.) 
Response: 
आपको अपना कामकरने का तरीका बदलना होगा। 
(You have to change the way you work.) 
Example 3 
User Request: 
िदवाली के िलए बधाई संदेश िलखें। 
(Write a greeting message for Diwali.) 
Response: आप सभी को िदवाली की हािद र्क शुभकामनाए ं । 
(Happy Diwali to all of you) 
Spelling Rationale 1: Words of Persian/Urdu origin must use 
the Nuqta (◌़) to represent the correct /z/ and /kh/ 
sounds. The correct spellings are ख़ास and िज़ंदगी. 
Missing the nuqta is a localization error in high-
quality hi_IN content. 
Rationale 2: This is a word-formation error. In Hindi, 
a verb phrase consisting of a noun/verb and its 
auxiliary (like kaam and karne) must be written as 
two separate words. The correct form is काम करने. 
Rationale 3: For feminine plural endings in formal 
hi_IN localization, the use of the Chandrabindu over 
the vowel (एँ)is the required standard. The correct 
spelling is शुभकामनाए ँ.

hi_IN Example 1 
User Request: 
iPhone 15 की कीमत क्या है? 
Response: भारत में iPhone 15 की शुरुआती कीमत 79,900 भारतीय रुपये (INR) है। 
Example 2 
User Request: 
मुझे आधार काडर् अपडेट करना है, क्या करना होगा? 
Response: आधार काडर्, जो भारत सरकार द्वारा जारी िकया गया एक 12-अंकों वाला 
पहचान पत्र है, उसे अपडेट करने के िलए आपको UIDAI की वेबसाइट पर जाना होगा। 
Example 3 
User Request: 
ताजमहल कहाँ िस्थत है? 
Response: ताजमहल भारत देश के उत्तर प्रदेश राज्य के आगरा शहर में िस्थत है। 
Non-local 
Perspective
Rationale 1: For a user in the hi_IN locale, specifying 
"Indian Rupees" or adding the currency code "(INR)" 
is unnecessary. It should simply be stated as 
₹79,900 or 79,900 रुपये. Specifying the currency as 
if it were a foreign exchange transaction makes the 
model seem outside the norm. 
Rationale 2: Providing a definition of an "Aadhar 
Card" is unnecessary for an Indian user. It draws 
attention to the user's locale in a way that implies 
the assistant is looking at the document from an 
outsider's perspective. 
Rationale 3: Phrases like "भारत देश के" (of the country 
India) are unnecessary. A local assistant should 
simply say "आगरा, उत्तर प्रदेश में िस्थत है।" Specifying the 
country makes the response feel like a translation for 
a global audience rather than a local one.

hi_IN Example 1 
User Request: 
अगले फ ु टबॉल मैच का समय क्या है? 
Response: भारत और क ु वैत के बीच सॉकर मैच कल शाम 7 बजे शुरू होगा। 
Example 2 
User Request: 
गमीर् बहुत है, कु छ ठंडा पीने का मन है। 
Response: आप एक मृदु पेय (Mridu Pey) पी सकते हैं।
Rationale 1: In the Indian context (hi_IN), the term 
फ़ुटबॉल is universally used. Using the word सॉकर 
(Soccer) is an Americanism that feels completely 
alien and unlocalized for an Indian user. 
Rationale 2: मृदु पेय is a literal, highly formal 
translation for "Soft Drink." No native speaker uses 
this in a casual context. A localized response should 
use कोल्ड िड्रंक or a specific name like िशक ं जी. 
hi_IN Example 1 
User Request: 
िदल्ली से जयपुर की दूरी िकतनी है? 
Response: िदल्ली सेजयपुर लगभग 175 मील दूर है। 
Units of 
measurement
Rationale 1: India uses the metric system. Distances 
between cities must always be provided in िकलोमीटर 
(km). Using "Miles" is a critical localization failure.

hi_IN Example 2 
User Request: 
भारत की जनसंख्या िकतनी है? 
Response: भारत की अनुमािनत जनसंख्या 1.4 िबिलयन है। 
Example 3 
User Request: 
आज मुझे तेज़ बुखार है, थमार्मीटर 101 िदखा रहा है। 
Response: 101°C बहुत तेज़ बुखार है, आपको तुरंत डॉक्टर को िदखाना चािहए। 
Units of 
measurement 
Rationale 2: While "Billion" is understood by 
educated users, the native numbering system for 
hi_IN is Lakh/Crore. A localized response should say 
140 करोड़. 
Rationale 3: While India uses Celsius for weather, 
Fahrenheit (°F) is the standard for body temperature. 
More importantly, 101°C is boiling point; the assistant 
failed to recognize the unit context. 
hi_IN Example 1: 
User Request: 
चाय क ै सी बनी है? 
Response:यह चाय बहुत अच्छा है। 
Example 2: 
User Request: 
सरकार की योजनाओं के बारे में बताए ं । 
Response:यह योजना मिहलाए ं के िलए बनाई गई है। 
Grammar Rationale 1: In Hindi, "Chai" (T ea) is a feminine noun. 
The adjective must agree with the gender of the 
noun. Using the masculine adjective "अच्छा" is a 
grammatical error. The correct phrase is "अच्छी है”.  
Rationale 2: This is a classic oblique case error. 
When a plural noun is followed by a postposition like 
"ke liye" (for), the noun must change its form. 
"मिहलाए ं" (Women - direct plural) is incorrect here; it 
must be inflected to "मिहलाओं" (Mahilaon). Correct: 
मिहलाओं के िलए.

hi_IN Example 3: 
User Request: 
महात्मा गांधी कौन थे? 
Response:महात्मा गांधी भारत के राष्ट्रिपता था। 
Grammar Rationale 3: In Hindi grammar, respect is shown 
through pluralization. Historical figures, elders, and 
formal subjects require the plural verb form even if 
they are singular individuals. Using "था" (was - 
singular/casual) is grammatically incorrect for a 
figure like Gandhi. It must be "थे" (were - respectful).

Example 3: 
User Request: 
क्या आप मुझे कल िमल सकतेहैं? 
Response: मैं आपसे सुनने के िलए आगे देख रहा हूँ। 
Awkward or 
Unnatural 
Writing
Rationale 1: The assistant literally translated the 
English idiom "Piece of cake" to "के क का एक टुकड़ा". 
This idiom does not exist in Hindi. A native speaker 
would say "बाए ँ हाथका खेल" (left hand's game) or 
simply "बहुत आसान" (very easy). 
Rationale 2: The word "बचाना" (Bachana) means "to 
save" in the sense of rescuing someone from danger 
(like saving a life). For saving a digital file, Hindi 
speakers use the loanword सेव करें (Save karein) or 
the formal सुरिक्षत करें (Surakshit karein). Using 
"Bachana" for a file sounds funny and unnatural. 
Rationale 3: This is a direct, clunky translation of the 
English phrase "I am looking forward to hearing from 
you." It sounds nonsensical in Hindi. A natural 
response would be "मैं आपक े जवाबका इं तज़ार करूँगा" (I 
will wait for your reply) or "आपसे बात करने की उत्सुकता 
रहेगी".

hi_IN Example 1: 
User Request: 
ताज महल के बारे में एक वाक्य िलखें। 
Response: ताज महल दुिनया के सात अजूबों में से एक है. 
Example 2: 
User Request: 
भारत की राजधानी क्या है? 
Response: भारत की राजधानी नई िदल्ली है ।यह एक बहुत बड़ा शहर है। 
Example 3: 
User Request: 
कछुआ क ै से चलता है? 
Response: कछुआ बहुत धीरे धीरे चलता है। 
Example 4: 
User Request: 
गांधी जयंती कब है? 
Response:गांधी जयंती 10/02 को मनाई जाती है। 
Formatting 
and 
Punctuation
Rationale 1: In Hindi Devanagari, the full stop is 
represented by a vertical line called the Purna Viram 
(।). Using a Latin period/dot (.) at the end of a 
sentence is a formatting error. The correct sentence 
should end with: ...में से एक है। 
Rationale 2: There is an incorrect space before the 
Purna Viram in the first sentence ("है।"). Punctuation 
marks in Hindi follow the same spacing rules as 
English: no space before the mark, and one space 
after it. It should be: ...नई िदल्ली है। 
Rationale 3: Hindi uses reduplicative (echo) words 
to emphasize meaning. These pairs must be 
connected by a hyphen. Writing them as separate 
words is a punctuation error. It should be: धीरे-धीरे. 
(Other examples: कभी-कभी, साथ-साथ). 
Rationale 4: The assistant used the US date format 
(MM/DD), which is ambiguous in the hi_IN locale. In 
India, the standard numerical date format is DD/MM. 
T o an Indian user, "10/02" reads as February 10th, 
not October 2nd. The response should use 02/10 or 
explicit text like 2 अक्टूबर to follow local formatting 
conventions.
